{tr.stats.network}.{tr.stats.station}.{tr.stats.channel}\")\n\n    try:\n        # SeisBench expects 3-component data, but can work with single traces\n        # Create a stream with just this trace\n traces to avoid duplicates\n        # PhaseNet works best with Z component for P/S picks\n        if not tr.stats.channel.endswith('Z'):\n            continue\n\n        # Annotate with the model (picks P and S phases)\n        annotations = model.annotate(trace_stream)\n\n        # Extract picks from annotations\n        picks = annotations.picks\n\n        for pick in picks:\n            pick_dict = {\n                'network': tr.stats.network,\n                'station': tr.stats.station,\n                'channel': tr.stats.channel,\n                'phase': pick.phase,  # 'P' or 'S'\n                'time': pick.peak_time.datetime.isoformat(),\n                'timestamp': pick.peak_time.timestamp,\n                'probability': pick.peak_value\n            }\n            picks_list.append(pick_dict)\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error processing trace {tr.stats.network}.{tr.stats.station}.{tr.stats.channel}: {e}\")\n        continue\n\npicks_df =  stations_gamma.append({\n        'station_id': station_id,\n        'latitude': info['latitude'],\n        'longitude': info['longitude'],\n        'elevation_m': info['elevation_m']\n 50],  # search range\n    'y(km)': [-50, 50],\n    'z(km)': [0, 30],    # depth range\n    'vel': {'p': 6.0, 's': 6.0/1.75},  # P and S wave velocities\n    'method': 'BGMM',  # Bayesian Gaussian Mixture Model\n    'oversample_factor': 4,\n    'dbscan_eps': 10.0,  # km\n    'dbscan_min_samples': 3,\n}\n\n# Initialize associator\nprint(\"\\nInitializing GaMMA associator...\")\ngamma_associator = associator.GaMMA(\n    config=config,\n    stations=stations_gamma_df,\n    picks=picks_gamma_df\n)\n\n# Run association\nprint(\"Running GaMMA phase association...\")\nevents, assignments = gamma_associator.run()\n\nprint(f\"\\nFound {len(events)} earthquake events\")\n\n# Convert events to required output format\nresults = []\nfor idx, event in events.iterrows():\n    event_dict = {\n Load waveform and station data\n2. Pick P and S waves using SeisBench\n3. Associate picks to identify earthquake events using GaMMA\n4. Write results to CSV\n\"\"\"\n\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom obspy import read, UTCDateTime\nimport seisbench.models as sbm\nimport seisbench.data as sbd\nfrom datetime import datetime\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\n# Load waveform data\nprint(\"Loading waveform data...\")\nst = read('/root/data/wave.mseed')\nprint(f\"Loaded {len(st)} traces\")\nprint(f\"Time range: {st[0].stats.starttime} to {st[0].stats.endtime}\")\n\n# Load station data\nprint(\"\\nLoading station data...\")\nstations_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/stations.csv')\nprint(f\"Loaded {len(stations_df)} station-channel entries\")\n\n# Create a station lookup dictionary (one entry per station, not per channel)\nstations_unique = stations_df.drop_duplicates(subset=['network', 'station'])\nprint(f\"Unique stations: {len(stations_unique)}\")\n\n# Load SeisBench picking model - using PhaseNet\nprint(\"\\nLoading SeisBench PhaseNet model...\")\nmodel = sbm.PhaseNet.from_pretrained(\"stead\")\nprint(\"Model loaded successfully\")\n\n# Prepare picks list\npicks_list = []\n\n# Process each station (not each trace, to get 3-component data)\nprint(\"\\nPerforming phase picking on stations...\")\nstations_processed = set()\n\nfor tr in st:\n    station_key = (tr.stats.network, tr.stats.station, tr.stats.location)\n\n    if station_key in stations_processed:\n        continue\n\n    stations_processed.add(station_key)\n\n    # PhaseNet expects 3 components\n    component_sets = [\n        ['HHE', 'HHN', 'HHZ'],\n        ['HNE', 'HNN', 'HNZ'],\n        ['BHE', 'BHN', 'BHZ'],\n        ['EHE', 'EHN', 'EHZ']\n    ]\n\n    for components in component_sets:\n        stream_3c = station_stream.copy()\n        # Select only these components\n        for comp in components:\n            if not any(tr.stats.channel == comp for tr in stream_3c):\n                stream_3c = None\n                break\n\n        if stream_3c is not None and len(stream_3c.select(channel=components[0])) > 0:\n            # We found a valid 3-component set\n            stream_3c = stream_3c.select(channel=components[0]) + \\\n                       stream_3c.select(channel=components[1]) + \\\n                       stream_3c.select(channel=components[2])\n\n            if len(stream_3c) >= 3:\n                break\n    else:\n        # Association Pipeline\n1. Load waveform and station data\n2. Pick P and S waves using SeisBench\n3. Associate picks to identify earthquake events using GaMMA\n4. Write results to CSV\n\"\"\"\n\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom obspy import read, UTCDateTime\nimport seisbench.models as sbm\nimport seisbench.data as sbd\nfrom datetime import datetime\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\n# Load waveform data\nprint(\"Loading waveform data...\")\nst = read('/root/data/wave.mseed')\nprint(f\"Loaded {len(st)} traces\")\nprint(f\"Time range: {st[0].stats.starttime} to {st[0].stats.endtime}\")\n\n# Load station data\nprint(\"\\nLoading station data...\")\nstations_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/stations.csv')\nprint(f\"Loaded {len(stations_df)} station-channel entries\")\n\n# Create a station lookup dictionary (one entry per station, not per channel)\nstations_unique = stations_df.drop_duplicates(subset=['network', 'station'])\nprint(f\"Unique stations: {len(stations_unique)}\")\n\n# Load SeisBench picking model - using PhaseNet\nprint(\"\\nLoading SeisBench PhaseNet model...\")\nmodel = sbm.PhaseNet.from_pretrained(\"stead\")\nprint(\"Model loaded successfully\")\n\n# Prepare picks list\npicks_list = []\n\n# Process each station (not each trace, to get 3-component data)\nprint(\"\\nPerforming phase picking on stations...\")\nstations_processed = set()\n\nfor tr in st:\n    station_key = (tr.stats.network, tr.stats.station, tr.stats.location)\n\n    if station_key in stations_processed:\n        continue\n\n    stations_processed.add(station_key)\n\n    # Get all traces for this station\n    station_stream = st.select(\n        network=tr.stats.network,\n        station=tr.stats.station,\n        location=tr.stats.location\n    )\n\n    # Try to get 3 component data (HHE, HHN, HHZ or HNE, HNN, HNZ)\n    # PhaseNet expects 3 components\n    component_sets = [\n        ['HHE', 'HHN', 'HHZ'],\n        ['HNE', 'HNN', 'HNZ'],\n        ['BHE', 'BHN', 'BHZ'],\n        ['EHE', 'EHN', 'EHZ']\n    ]\n\n    for components in component_sets:\n        stream_3c = station_stream.copy()\n        # Select only these components\n        for comp in components:\n            if not any(tr.stats.channel == comp for tr in stream_3c):\n                stream_3c = None\n                break\n\n        if stream_3c is not None and len(stream_3c.select(channel=components[0])) > 0:\n            # We found a valid 3-component set\n            stream_3c = stream_3c.select(channel=components[0]) + \\\n                       stream_3c.select(channel=components[1]) + \\\n                       stream_3c.select(channel=components[2])\n\n            if len(stream_3c) >= 3:\n                break\n    else:\n        # No 3-component data found, skip\n        continue\n\n    print(f\"Processing station {tr.stats.network}.{tr.stats.station}\")\n\n    try:\n        # Annotate with the model - returns probability traces\n        annotations = model.annotate(stream_3c)\n\n        # Extract picks using SeisBench's picker\n        # The annotations contain probability traces for P, S, and Noise\n        # We need to find peaks in these traces\n        from scipy.signal import find_peaks\n\n        for ann_tr in annotations:\n            if 'P' in ann_tr.stats.channel or ann_tr.stats.channel == 'PhaseNet_P':\n                # PhaseNet expects 3 components\n    component_sets = [\n        ['HHE', 'HHN', 'HHZ'],\n        ['HNE', 'HNN', 'HNZ'],\n        ['BHE', 'BHN', 'BHZ'],\n        ['EHE', 'EHN', 'EHZ']\n    ]\n\n    for components in component_sets:\n        stream_3c = station_stream.copy()\n        # Select only these components\n        for comp in components:\n            if not any(tr.stats.channel == comp for tr in stream_3c):\n                stream_3c = None\n                break\n\n        if stream_3c is not None and len(stream_3c.select(channel=components[0])) > 0:\n            # We found a valid 3-component set\n            stream_3c = stream_3c.select(channel=components[0]) + \\\n                       stream_3c.select(channel=components[1]) + \\\n                       stream_3c.select(channel=components[2])\n\n            if len(stream_3c) >= 3:\n                break\n    else:\n        # No 3-component data found, skip\n        continue\n\n    print(f\"Processing station {tr.stats.network}.{tr.stats.station}\")\n\n    try:\n        # Annotate with the model - returns probability traces\n        annotations = model.annotate(stream_3c)\n\n        # Extract picks using SeisBench's picker\n        # The annotations contain probability traces for P, S, and Noise\n        # We need to find peaks in these traces\n        from scipy.signal import find_peaks\n\n        for ann_tr in annotations:\n            if 'P' in ann_tr.stats.channel or ann_tr.stats.channel == 'PhaseNet_P':\n                # This is the P-wave probability trace\n                prob_data = ann_tr.data\n                peaks, properties = find_peaks(prob_data, height=0.3, distance=100)\n\n                for peak_idx in peaks:\n                    peak_time = ann_tr.stats.starttime + peak_idx * ann_tr.stats.delta\n                    peak_prob = prob_data[peak_idx]\n\n                    picks_list.append({\n                        'network': tr.stats.network,\n                        'station': tr.stats.station,\n                        'phase': 'P',\n                        'time': peak_time.datetime.isoformat(),\n                        'timestamp': peak_time.timestamp,\n                        'probability': peak_prob\n                    })\n\n            elif 'S' in ann_tr.stats.channel or ann_tr.stats.channel == 'PhaseNet_S':\n                # This is the S-wave probability trace\n                prob_data = ann_tr.data\n                peaks, properties = find_peaks(prob_data, height=0.3, distance=100)\n\n                for peak_idx in peaks:\n                    peak_time = ann_tr.stats.starttime + peak_idx * ann_tr.stats.delta\n                    peak_prob = prob_data[peak_idx]\n\n                    picks_list.append({\n                        'network': tr.stats.network,\n                        'station': tr.stats.station,\n                        'phase': 'S',\n                        'time': peak_time.datetime.isoformat(),\n                        'timestamp': peak_time.timestamp,\n                        'probability': peak_prob\n                    })\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error processing station {tr.stats.network}.{tr.stats.station}: {e}\")\n        import traceback\n        traceback.print_exc()\n        continue\n\npicks_df = pd.DataFrame(picks_list)\nprint(f\"\\nTotal picks: {len(picks_df)}\")\nif len(picks_df) > 0:\n    print(f\"P picks: {len(picks_df[picks_df['phase'] == 'P'])}\")\n    print(f\"S picks: {len(picks_df[picks_df['phase'] == 'S'])}\")\n\n    # Save picks for debugging\n    picks_df.to_csv('/root/picks_debug.csv', index=False)\n    print(\"Picks saved to /root/picks_debug.csv\")\nelse:\n    print(\"No picks found! Cannot proceed with association.\")\n    # Create empty results file\n    pd.DataFrame({'time': []}).to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\n    exit(1)\n\n# Now use PyOcto for phase association\nprint(\"\\nPreparing data for PyOcto phase association...\")\n\n# Import PyOcto\nfrom pyocto import OctoAssociator, VelocityModel0D\nfrom pyocto._core import Station, Pick\n\n# Calculate center point and bounds for search space\ncenter_lat = stations_unique['latitude'].mean()\ncenter_lon = stations_unique['longitude'].mean()\n\n# Create velocity model (constant velocity)\n# vp = 6 km/s, vs = 6/1.75 km/s\nvelocity_model = VelocityModel0D(\n    p_velocity=6.0,  # km/s\n    s_velocity=6.0/1.75,  # km/s\n    tolerance=1.0,  # seconds\n    association_cutoff_distance=100.0,  # km\n    location_cutoff_distance=100.0  # km\n)\n\n# Create Station objects\nstations_pyocto = []\nfor _, row in stations_unique.iterrows():\n    station = Station(\n        id=f\"{row['network']}.{row['station']}\",\n        x=row['longitude'],\n        y=row['latitude'],\n        z=-row['elevation_m'] / 1000.0  # convert to km, negative for depth convention\n    )\n    stations_pyocto.append(station)\n\nprint(f\"Created {len(stations_pyocto)} station objects\")\n\n# Create Pick objects\npicks_pyocto = []\nfor _, pick_row in picks_df.iterrows():\n    station_id = f\"{pick_row['network']}.{pick_row['station']}\"\n    # Convert timestamp to datetime\n    pick_time = pick_row['timestamp']\n\n    pick = Pick(\n        station_id=station_id,\n        phase=pick_row['phase'],\n        time=pick_time,\n        uncertainty=1.0  # seconds\n    )\n    picks_pyocto.append(pick)\n\nprint(f\"Created {len(picks_pyocto)} pick objects\")\n\n# Define search space bounds (in lon/lat/depth km)\n# Estimate bounds based on station locations\nlon_range = stations_unique['longitude'].max() - stations_unique['longitude'].min()\nlat_range = stations_unique['latitude'].max() - stations_unique['latitude'].min()\n\nxlim = (center_lon - lon_range, center_lon + lon_range)\nylim = (center_lat - lat_range, center_lat + lat_range)\nzlim = (0, 30)  # depth range in km\n\nprint(f\"Search space: x={xlim}, y={ylim}, z={zlim}\")\n\n# Initialize PyOcto associator\nprint(\"\\nInitializing PyOcto associator...\")\ntry:\n    associator = OctoAssociator(\n        xlim=xlim,\n        ylim=ylim,\n        zlim=zlim,\n        velocity_model=velocity_model,\n        time_before=30.0,  # seconds before first pick to start search\n        min_node_size=10.0,  # km\n        min_pick_fraction=0.25,\n        n_picks=5,  # minimum picks per event\n        n_p_picks=3,  # minimum P picks\n        n_s_picks=0,  # minimum S picks (we have mostly P picks)\n        n_p_and_s_picks=0,  # minimum P+S picks\n        refinement_iterations=3\n    )\n\n    # Run association\n    print(\"Running PyOcto phase association...\")\n    events, assignments = associator.associate(picks_pyocto, stations_pyocto)\n\n    print(f\"Found {len(events)} earthquake events\")\n\n    # Convert events to required output format\n    results = []\n    for event in events:\n        # Event object has attributes: time, x (lon), y (lat), z (depth)\n        event_dict = {\n            'time': datetime.utcfromtimestamp(event.time).isoformat(),\n            'timestamp': event.time,\n            'longitude': event.x,\n            'latitude': event.y,\n            'depth_km': event.z,\n            'num_picks': len([a for a in assignments if a.event_idx == event.idx]) if hasattr(event, 'idx') else 0\n        }\n        results.append(event_dict)\n\n    results_df = pd.DataFrame(results)\n\n    # Write results to CSV\n        results_df.to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\n        print(f\"\\nResults written to /root/results.csv\")\n        print(f\"Total events identified: {len(results_df)}\")\n    else:\n        print(\"No events found!\")\n        pd.DataFrame({'time': []}).to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\n\nexcept Exception as e:\n    print(f\"Error during PyOcto association: {e}\")\n    import traceback\n    traceback.print_exc()\n    # Get all traces for this station\n    station_stream = st.select(\n        network=tr.stats.network,\n        station=tr.stats.station,\n        location=tr.stats.location\n    )\n\n    # Try to get 3 component data (HHE, HHN, HHZ or HNE, HNN, HNZ)\n    # PhaseNet expects 3 components\n    component_sets = [\n        ['HHE', 'HHN', 'HHZ'],\n        ['HNE', 'HNN', 'HNZ'],\n        ['BHE', 'BHN', 'BHZ'],\n        ['EHE', 'EHN', 'EHZ']\n    ]\n\n    for components in component_sets:\n        stream_3c = station_stream.copy()\n        # Select only these components\n        for comp in components:\n            if not any(tr.stats.channel == comp for tr in stream_3c):\n                stream_3c = None\n                break\n\n        if stream_3c is not None and len(stream_3c.select(channel=components[0])) > 0:\n            # This is the P-wave probability trace\n                prob_data = ann_tr.data\n                peaks, properties = find_peaks(prob_data, height=0.3, distance=100)\n\n                for peak_idx in peaks:\n                    peak_time = ann_tr.stats.starttime + peak_idx * ann_tr.stats.delta\n                    peak_prob = prob_data[peak_idx]\n\n                    picks_list.append({\n                        'network': tr.stats.network,\n                        'station': tr.stats.station,\n                        'phase': 'P',\n                        'time': peak_time.datetime.isoformat(),\n                        'timestamp': peak_time.timestamp,\n                        'probability': peak_prob\n                    })\n\n            elif 'S' in ann_tr.stats.channel or ann_tr.stats.channel == 'PhaseNet_S':\n                # id\n        row['longitude'],  # x\n        row['latitude'],  # y\n        0.0,  # z (depth, typically 0 for surface stations)\n        row['elevation_m'] / 1000.0,  # elevation_p in km\n        row['elevation_m'] / 1000.0   # Get all traces for this station\n    station_stream = st.select(\n        network=tr.stats.network,\n        station=tr.stats.station,\n        location=tr.stats.location\n    )\n\n    # Try to get 3 component data (HHE, HHN, HHZ or HNE, HNN, HNZ)\n    # PhaseNet expects 3 components\n    component_sets = [\n        ['HHE', 'HHN', 'HHZ'],\n        ['HNE', 'HNN', 'HNZ'],\n        ['BHE', 'BHN', 'BHZ'],\n        ['EHE', 'EHN', 'EHZ']\n    ]\n\n    for components in component_sets:\n        stream_3c = station_stream.copy()\n        # Select only these components\n        for comp in components:\n            if not any(tr.stats.channel == comp for tr in stream_3c):\n                stream_3c = None\n                break\n\n        if stream_3c is not None and len(stream_3c.select(channel=components[0])) > 0:\n            # We found a valid 3-component set\n            stream_3c = stream_3c.select(channel=components[0]) + \\\n                       stream_3c.select(channel=components[1]) + \\\n                       stream_3c.select(channel=components[2])\n\n            if len(stream_3c) >= 3:\n                break\n    else:\n        # No 3-component data found, skip\n        continue\n\n    print(f\"Processing station {tr.stats.network}.{tr.stats.station}\")\n\n    try:\n        # Annotate with the model - returns probability traces\n        annotations = model.annotate(stream_3c)\n\n        # Extract picks using SeisBench's picker\n        # The annotations contain probability traces for P, S, and Noise\n        # We need to find peaks in these traces\n        from scipy.signal import find_peaks\n\n        for ann_tr in annotations:\n            if 'P' in ann_tr.stats.channel or ann_tr.stats.channel == 'PhaseNet_P':\n                # This is the P-wave probability trace\n                prob_data = ann_tr.data\n                peaks, properties = find_peaks(prob_data, height=0.3, distance=100)\n\n                for peak_idx in peaks:\n                    peak_time = ann_tr.stats.starttime + peak_idx * ann_tr.stats.delta\n                    peak_prob = prob_data[peak_idx]\n\n                    picks_list.append({\n                        'network': tr.stats.network,\n                        'station': tr.stats.station,\n                        'phase': 'P',\n                        'time': peak_time.datetime.isoformat(),\n                        'timestamp': peak_time.timestamp,\n                        'probability': peak_prob\n                    })\n\n            elif 'S' in ann_tr.stats.channel or ann_tr.stats.channel == 'PhaseNet_S':\n                # id\n        row['longitude'],  # x\n        row['latitude'],  # y\n        0.0,  # z (depth, typically 0 for surface stations)\n        row['elevation_m'] / 1000.0,  # elevation_p in km\n        row['elevation_m'] / 1000.0   # elevation_s in km\n    )\n    stations_pyocto.append(station)\n\nprint(f\"Created {len(stations_pyocto)} station objects\")\n\n# Create Pick objects\n# First create a station_id to index mapping\nstation_id_to_idx = {f\"{row.network}.{row.station}\": idx\n                      for idx, row in enumerate(stations_unique.itertuples())}\n\npicks_pyocto = []\nfor _, pick_row in picks_df.iterrows():\n    station_id = f\"{pick_row['network']}.{pick_row['station']}\"\n    if station_id not in station_id_to_idx:\n        continue\n\n    station_idx = station_id_to_idx[station_id]\n    pick_time = pick_row['timestamp']\n\n    # depth range in km\n\nprint(f\"Search space: x={xlim}, y={ylim}, z={zlim}\")\n\n# Initialize PyOcto associator\nprint(\"\\nInitializing PyOcto associator...\")\ntry:\n    associator = OctoAssociator(\n        xlim=xlim,\n        ylim=ylim,\n        zlim=zlim,\n        velocity_model=velocity_model,\n        time_before=30.0,  # minimum S picks (we have mostly P picks)\n        n_p_and_s_picks=0,  # minimum P+S picks\n        refinement_iterations=3\n    )\n\n    # Run association\n    print(\"Running PyOcto phase association...\")\n    events, assignments = associator.associate(picks_pyocto, stations_pyocto)\n\n    print(f\"Found {len(events)} earthquake events\")\n\n    # Load waveform and station data\n2. Pick P and S waves using SeisBench\n3. Associate picks to identify earthquake events using GaMMA\n4. Write results to CSV\n\"\"\"\n\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom obspy import read, UTCDateTime\nimport seisbench.models as sbm\nimport seisbench.data as sbd\nfrom datetime import datetime\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\n# Load waveform data\nprint(\"Loading waveform data...\")\nst = read('/root/data/wave.mseed')\nprint(f\"Loaded {len(st)} traces\")\nprint(f\"Time range: {st[0].stats.starttime} to {st[0].stats.endtime}\")\n\n# Load station data\nprint(\"\\nLoading station data...\")\nstations_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/stations.csv')\nprint(f\"Loaded {len(stations_df)} station-channel entries\")\n\n# Create a station lookup dictionary (one entry per station, not per channel)\nstations_unique = stations_df.drop_duplicates(subset=['network', 'station'])\nprint(f\"Unique stations: {len(stations_unique)}\")\n\n# Load SeisBench picking model - using PhaseNet\nprint(\"\\nLoading SeisBench PhaseNet model...\")\nmodel = sbm.PhaseNet.from_pretrained(\"stead\")\nprint(\"Model loaded successfully\")\n\n# Prepare picks list\npicks_list = []\n\n# Process each station (not each trace, to get 3-component data)\nprint(\"\\nPerforming phase picking on stations...\")\nstations_processed = set()\n\nfor tr in st:\n    station_key = (tr.stats.network, tr.stats.station, tr.stats.location)\n\n    if station_key in stations_processed:\n        continue\n\n    stations_processed.add(station_key)\n\n    # Get all traces for this station\n    station_stream = st.select(\n        network=tr.stats.network,\n        station=tr.stats.station,\n        location=tr.stats.location\n    )\n\n    # Try to get 3 component data (HHE, HHN, HHZ or HNE, HNN, HNZ)\n    # PhaseNet expects 3 components\n    component_sets = [\n        ['HHE', 'HHN', 'HHZ'],\n        ['HNE', 'HNN', 'HNZ'],\n        ['BHE', 'BHN', 'BHZ'],\n        ['EHE', 'EHN', 'EHZ']\n    ]\n\n    for components in component_sets:\n        stream_3c = station_stream.copy()\n        # Select only these components\n        for comp in components:\n            if not any(tr.stats.channel == comp for tr in stream_3c):\n                stream_3c = None\n                break\n\n        if stream_3c is not None and len(stream_3c.select(channel=components[0])) > 0:\n            # We found a valid 3-component set\n            stream_3c = stream_3c.select(channel=components[0]) + \\\n                       stream_3c.select(channel=components[1]) + \\\n                       stream_3c.select(channel=components[2])\n\n            if len(stream_3c) >= 3:\n                break\n    else:\n        # No 3-component data found, skip\n        continue\n\n    print(f\"Processing station {tr.stats.network}.{tr.stats.station}\")\n\n    try:\n        # Annotate with the model - returns probability traces\n        annotations = model.annotate(stream_3c)\n\n        # Extract picks using SeisBench's picker\n        # The annotations contain probability traces for P, S, and Noise\n        # We need to find peaks in these traces\n        from scipy.signal import find_peaks\n\n        for ann_tr in annotations:\n            if 'P' in ann_tr.stats.channel or ann_tr.stats.channel == 'PhaseNet_P':\n                # This is the P-wave probability trace\n                prob_data = ann_tr.data\n                peaks, properties = find_peaks(prob_data, height=0.3, distance=100)\n\n                for peak_idx in peaks:\n                    peak_time = ann_tr.stats.starttime + peak_idx * ann_tr.stats.delta\n                    peak_prob = prob_data[peak_idx]\n\n                    picks_list.append({\n                        'network': tr.stats.network,\n                        'station': tr.stats.station,\n                        'phase': 'P',\n                        'time': peak_time.datetime.isoformat(),\n                        'timestamp': peak_time.timestamp,\n                        'probability': peak_prob\n                    })\n\n            elif 'S' in ann_tr.stats.channel or ann_tr.stats.channel == 'PhaseNet_S':\n                # This is the S-wave probability trace\n                prob_data = ann_tr.data\n                peaks, properties = find_peaks(prob_data, height=0.3, distance=100)\n\n                for peak_idx in peaks:\n                    peak_time = ann_tr.stats.starttime + peak_idx * ann_tr.stats.delta\n                    peak_prob = prob_data[peak_idx]\n\n                    picks_list.append({\n                        'network': tr.stats.network,\n                        'station': tr.stats.station,\n                        'phase': 'S',\n                        'time': peak_time.datetime.isoformat(),\n                        'timestamp': peak_time.timestamp,\n                        'probability': peak_prob\n                    })\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error processing station {tr.stats.network}.{tr.stats.station}: {e}\")\n        import traceback\n        traceback.print_exc()\n        continue\n\npicks_df = pd.DataFrame(picks_list)\nprint(f\"\\nTotal picks: {len(picks_df)}\")\nif len(picks_df) > 0:\n    print(f\"P picks: {len(picks_df[picks_df['phase'] == 'P'])}\")\n    print(f\"S picks: {len(picks_df[picks_df['phase'] == 'S'])}\")\n\n    # km/s\n    s_velocity=6.0/1.75,  # km/s\n    tolerance=1.0,  # seconds\n    association_cutoff_distance=100.0,  # km\n    location_cutoff_distance=100.0  # km\n)\n\n# Create Station objects\n# Station(id, x, y, z, elevation_p, elevation_s)\nstations_pyocto = []\nfor _, row in stations_unique.iterrows():\n    station = Station(\n        f\"{row['network']}.{row['station']}\",  # id\n        row['longitude'],  # x\n        row['latitude'],  # y\n        0.0,  # z (depth, typically 0 for surface stations)\n        row['elevation_m'] / 1000.0,  # elevation_p in km\n        row['elevation_m'] / 1000.0   # depth range in km\n\nprint(f\"Search space: x={xlim}, y={ylim}, z={zlim}\")\n\n# Initialize PyOcto associator\nprint(\"\\nInitializing PyOcto associator...\")\ntry:\n    associator = OctoAssociator(\n        xlim=xlim,\n        ylim=ylim,\n        zlim=zlim,\n        velocity_model=velocity_model,\n        time_before=30.0,  # We found a valid 3-component set\n            stream_3c = stream_3c.select(channel=components[0]) + \\\n                       stream_3c.select(channel=components[1]) + \\\n                       stream_3c.select(channel=components[2])\n\n            if len(stream_3c) >= 3:\n                break\n    else:\n        # Extract picks using SeisBench's picker\n        # The annotations contain probability traces for P, S, and Noise\n        # We need to find peaks in these traces\n        from scipy.signal import find_peaks\n\n        for ann_tr in annotations:\n            if 'P' in ann_tr.stats.channel or ann_tr.stats.channel == 'PhaseNet_P':\n                # This is the P-wave probability trace\n                prob_data = ann_tr.data\n                peaks, properties = find_peaks(prob_data, height=0.3, distance=100)\n\n                for peak_idx in peaks:\n                    peak_time = ann_tr.stats.starttime + peak_idx * ann_tr.stats.delta\n                    peak_prob = prob_data[peak_idx]\n\n                    picks_list.append({\n                        'network': tr.stats.network,\n                        'station': tr.stats.station,\n                        'phase': 'P',\n                        'time': peak_time.datetime.isoformat(),\n                        'timestamp': peak_time.timestamp,\n                        'probability': peak_prob\n                    })\n\n            elif 'S' in ann_tr.stats.channel or ann_tr.stats.channel == 'PhaseNet_S':\n                # This is the S-wave probability trace\n                prob_data = ann_tr.data\n                peaks, properties = find_peaks(prob_data, height=0.3, distance=100)\n\n                for peak_idx in peaks:\n                    peak_time = ann_tr.stats.starttime + peak_idx * ann_tr.stats.delta\n                    peak_prob = prob_data[peak_idx]\n\n                    picks_list.append({\n                        'network': tr.stats.network,\n                        'station': tr.stats.station,\n                        'phase': 'S',\n                        'time': peak_time.datetime.isoformat(),\n                        'timestamp': peak_time.timestamp,\n                        'probability': peak_prob\n                    })\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error processing station {tr.stats.network}.{tr.stats.station}: {e}\")\n        import traceback\n        traceback.print_exc()\n        continue\n\npicks_df = pd.DataFrame(picks_list)\nprint(f\"\\nTotal picks: {len(picks_df)}\")\nif len(picks_df) > 0:\n    print(f\"P picks: {len(picks_df[picks_df['phase'] == 'P'])}\")\n    print(f\"S picks: {len(picks_df[picks_df['phase'] == 'S'])}\")\n\n    # km/s\n    s_velocity=6.0/1.75,  # km/s\n    tolerance=1.0,  # seconds\n    association_cutoff_distance=100.0,  # km\n    location_cutoff_distance=100.0  # km\n)\n\n# Create stations DataFrame in PyOcto format\n# Required columns: id, x (lon), y (lat), z (depth), elevation_p, elevation_s\nstations_pyocto = []\nfor _, row in stations_unique.iterrows():\n    stations_pyocto.append({\n        'id': f\"{row['network']}.{row['station']}\",\n        'x': row['longitude'],\n        'y': row['latitude'],\n        'z': 0.0,  # depth in km\n        'elevation_p': row['elevation_m'] / 1000.0,  # elevation in km\n        'elevation_s': row['elevation_m'] / 1000.0\n    })\n\nstations_pyocto_df = pd.DataFrame(stations_pyocto)\nprint(f\"Created stations DataFrame with {len(stations_pyocto_df)} stations\")\n\n# Create picks DataFrame in PyOcto format\n# Required columns: station_id, phase, time\npicks_pyocto = []\nfor _, pick_row in picks_df.iterrows():\n    station_id = f\"{pick_row['network']}.{pick_row['station']}\"\n    picks_pyocto.append({\n        'station_id': station_id,\n        'phase': pick_row['phase'],\n        'time': pick_row['timestamp']\n    })\n\npicks_pyocto_df = pd.DataFrame(picks_pyocto)\nprint(f\"Created picks DataFrame with {len(picks_pyocto_df)} picks\")\n\n# Define search space bounds (in lon/lat/depth km)\n# Estimate bounds based on station locations\nlon_range = stations_unique['longitude'].max() - stations_unique['longitude'].min()\nlat_range = stations_unique['latitude'].max() - stations_unique['latitude'].min()\n\nxlim = (center_lon - lon_range, center_lon + lon_range)\nylim = (center_lat - lat_range, center_lat + lat_range)\nzlim = (0, 30)  # depth range in km\n\nprint(f\"Search space: x={xlim}, y={ylim}, z={zlim}\")\n\n# Initialize PyOcto associator\nprint(\"\\nInitializing PyOcto associator...\")\ntry:\n    associator = OctoAssociator(\n        xlim=xlim,\n        ylim=ylim,\n        zlim=zlim,\n        velocity_model=velocity_model,\n        time_before=30.0,  # Load waveform and station data\n2. Pick P and S waves using SeisBench\n3. Associate picks to identify earthquake events using GaMMA\n4. Write results to CSV\n\"\"\"\n\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom obspy import read, UTCDateTime\nimport seisbench.models as sbm\nimport seisbench.data as sbd\nfrom datetime import datetime\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\n# Load waveform data\nprint(\"Loading waveform data...\")\nst = read('/root/data/wave.mseed')\nprint(f\"Loaded {len(st)} traces\")\nprint(f\"Time range: {st[0].stats.starttime} to {st[0].stats.endtime}\")\n\n# Load station data\nprint(\"\\nLoading station data...\")\nstations_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/stations.csv')\nprint(f\"Loaded {len(stations_df)} station-channel entries\")\n\n# Create a station lookup dictionary (one entry per station, not per channel)\nstations_unique = stations_df.drop_duplicates(subset=['network', 'station'])\nprint(f\"Unique stations: {len(stations_unique)}\")\n\n# Load SeisBench picking model - using PhaseNet\nprint(\"\\nLoading SeisBench PhaseNet model...\")\nmodel = sbm.PhaseNet.from_pretrained(\"stead\")\nprint(\"Model loaded successfully\")\n\n# Prepare picks list\npicks_list = []\n\n# Process each station (not each trace, to get 3-component data)\nprint(\"\\nPerforming phase picking on stations...\")\nstations_processed = set()\n\nfor tr in st:\n    station_key = (tr.stats.network, tr.stats.station, tr.stats.location)\n\n    if station_key in stations_processed:\n        continue\n\n    stations_processed.add(station_key)\n\n    # Get all traces for this station\n    station_stream = st.select(\n        network=tr.stats.network,\n        station=tr.stats.station,\n        location=tr.stats.location\n    )\n\n    # Try to get 3 component data (HHE, HHN, HHZ or HNE, HNN, HNZ)\n    # PhaseNet expects 3 components\n    component_sets = [\n        ['HHE', 'HHN', 'HHZ'],\n        ['HNE', 'HNN', 'HNZ'],\n        ['BHE', 'BHN', 'BHZ'],\n        ['EHE', 'EHN', 'EHZ']\n    ]\n\n    for components in component_sets:\n        stream_3c = station_stream.copy()\n        # Select only these components\n        for comp in components:\n            if not any(tr.stats.channel == comp for tr in stream_3c):\n                stream_3c = None\n                break\n\n        if stream_3c is not None and len(stream_3c.select(channel=components[0])) > 0:\n            # We found a valid 3-component set\n            stream_3c = stream_3c.select(channel=components[0]) + \\\n                       stream_3c.select(channel=components[1]) + \\\n                       stream_3c.select(channel=components[2])\n\n            if len(stream_3c) >= 3:\n                break\n    else:\n        # No 3-component data found, skip\n        continue\n\n    print(f\"Processing station {tr.stats.network}.{tr.stats.station}\")\n\n    try:\n        # Annotate with the model - returns probability traces\n        annotations = model.annotate(stream_3c)\n\n        # Extract picks using SeisBench's picker\n        # The annotations contain probability traces for P, S, and Noise\n        # We need to find peaks in these traces\n        from scipy.signal import find_peaks\n\n        for ann_tr in annotations:\n            if 'P' in ann_tr.stats.channel or ann_tr.stats.channel == 'PhaseNet_P':\n                # This is the P-wave probability trace\n                prob_data = ann_tr.data\n                peaks, properties = find_peaks(prob_data, height=0.3, distance=100)\n\n                for peak_idx in peaks:\n                    peak_time = ann_tr.stats.starttime + peak_idx * ann_tr.stats.delta\n                    peak_prob = prob_data[peak_idx]\n\n                    picks_list.append({\n                        'network': tr.stats.network,\n                        'station': tr.stats.station,\n                        'phase': 'P',\n                        'time': peak_time.datetime.isoformat(),\n                        'timestamp': peak_time.timestamp,\n                        'probability': peak_prob\n                    })\n\n            elif 'S' in ann_tr.stats.channel or ann_tr.stats.channel == 'PhaseNet_S':\n                # This is the S-wave probability trace\n                prob_data = ann_tr.data\n                peaks, properties = find_peaks(prob_data, height=0.3, distance=100)\n\n                for peak_idx in peaks:\n                    peak_time = ann_tr.stats.starttime + peak_idx * ann_tr.stats.delta\n                    peak_prob = prob_data[peak_idx]\n\n                    picks_list.append({\n                        'network': tr.stats.network,\n                        'station': tr.stats.station,\n                        'phase': 'S',\n                        'time': peak_time.datetime.isoformat(),\n                        'timestamp': peak_time.timestamp,\n                        'probability': peak_prob\n                    })\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error processing station {tr.stats.network}.{tr.stats.station}: {e}\")\n        import traceback\n        traceback.print_exc()\n        continue\n\npicks_df = pd.DataFrame(picks_list)\nprint(f\"\\nTotal picks: {len(picks_df)}\")\nif len(picks_df) > 0:\n    print(f\"P picks: {len(picks_df[picks_df['phase'] == 'P'])}\")\n    print(f\"S picks: {len(picks_df[picks_df['phase'] == 'S'])}\")\n\n    # km/s\n    s_velocity=6.0/1.75,  # km/s\n    tolerance=1.0,  # seconds\n    association_cutoff_distance=100.0,  # km\n    location_cutoff_distance=100.0  # km\n)\n\n# Create stations DataFrame in PyOcto format\n# Required columns: id, x (lon), y (lat), z (depth), elevation_p, elevation_s\nstations_pyocto = []\nfor _, row in stations_unique.iterrows():\n    stations_pyocto.append({\n        'id': f\"{row['network']}.{row['station']}\",\n        'x': row['longitude'],\n        'y': row['latitude'],\n        'z': 0.0,  # depth in km\n        'elevation_p': row['elevation_m'] / 1000.0,  # elevation in km\n        'elevation_s': row['elevation_m'] / 1000.0\n    })\n\nstations_pyocto_df = pd.DataFrame(stations_pyocto)\nprint(f\"Created stations DataFrame with {len(stations_pyocto_df)} stations\")\n\n# Create picks DataFrame in PyOcto format\n# Required columns: station, phase, time\npicks_pyocto = []\nfor _, pick_row in picks_df.iterrows():\n    station_id = f\"{pick_row['network']}.{pick_row['station']}\"\n    picks_pyocto.append({\n        'station': station_id,\n        'phase': pick_row['phase'],\n        'time': pick_row['timestamp']\n    })\n\npicks_pyocto_df = pd.DataFrame(picks_pyocto)\nprint(f\"Created picks DataFrame with {len(picks_pyocto_df)} picks\")\n\n# Define search space bounds (in lon/lat/depth km)\n# Estimate bounds based on station locations\nlon_range = stations_unique['longitude'].max() - stations_unique['longitude'].min()\nlat_range = stations_unique['latitude'].max() - stations_unique['latitude'].min()\n\nxlim = (center_lon - lon_range, center_lon + lon_range)\nylim = (center_lat - lat_range, center_lat + lat_range)\nzlim = (0, 30)  # depth range in km\n\nprint(f\"Search space: x={xlim}, y={ylim}, z={zlim}\")\n\n# Initialize PyOcto associator\nprint(\"\\nInitializing PyOcto associator...\")\ntry:\n    associator = OctoAssociator(\n        xlim=xlim,\n        ylim=ylim,\n        zlim=zlim,\n        velocity_model=velocity_model,\n        time_before=30.0,  #
